All memory returned by malloc() is guaranteed to be power of two aligned and won't span a page boundry if <= a page in size; there are many places in the kernel that rely on this. If you need a large chunk of memory that must be physically contiguous, then use contigmalloc(). -DG David Greenman Core

This starts a > kernel shutdown with the RB_POWEROFF flag set.
